# Font Match Research

Paid-font and Adobe Fonts research for the 145 custom family names in display-specs (11).json, followed by supporting research on their underlying Google Fonts and the complete liked-font inventory from font-lab-families (24).csv.

## Contents

- [Custom derived open-source families](#custom-derived-open-source-families)
- [Dataset summary](#dataset-summary)
- [Methodology and limitations](#methodology-and-limitations)
- [Top commercial upgrade routes](#top-commercial-upgrade-routes)
- [Consolidated match clusters](#consolidated-match-clusters)
- [Family-by-family inventory](#family-by-family-inventory)
- [Sources and verification](#sources-and-verification)

## Custom derived open-source families

These 145 custom family names are the primary entries in this report. Each name is listed first; its Google Font base is retained only as provenance and as the starting point for paid-font matching. The comparison setup uses the custom weight, tracking, case mode, width/slant axes, and other treatment settings from the JSON.

| Custom family | Based on | Name status | Open-source status | Treatment settings | Size scale | Metrics status | Match cluster | Closest Adobe Fonts option | Other paid option | Confidence | Paid comparison setup | Why it matches | Key difference / caveat | Sources |

| Metric | Value |

| Families marked like | 663 |

| High-confidence assignments | 486 |
| Medium or loose assignments | 177 |

| Combined source and custom entries | 808 |
| Unique displayed family names | 806 |

## Methodology and limitations

The file contains only family and verdict. It has no saved weight, width, case, tracking, or style labels, so the research compares family design rather than a specific treatment. Exact replacement still requires testing the intended weight, text, language, and size.

Confidence is assigned to the recommendation, not to the quality of the liked font:

| Confidence | Meaning | Recommended use |
| --- | --- | --- |
| High | Credible structural or historical substitute | Shortlist and test |
| Medium | Same family behavior or adjacent genre | Compare specimens |
| Loose | Search direction only | Do not substitute blindly |

> Important non-Latin rule: Arabic, Devanagari and other Indic scripts, Thai, Khmer, Hebrew, CJK, Korean, and other writing systems are routed by script. A Latin-only lookalike is not treated as a valid replacement. Native-reader review is still required for production use.

The 145 custom family names from display-specs (11).json are treated as open source because the user explicitly requested that assumption. This report does not independently determine or alter the license of the underlying font software.

Two custom names collide with existing Google Fonts family names: **Chewy** and **Ledger**. Their rows remain included but are flagged so the custom derivative is not confused with the unrelated Google family.

## Top commercial upgrade routes

These are the 28 clusters where a paid or Adobe Fonts family most clearly adds range, polish, historical specificity, or production depth.

## Consolidated match clusters

The 663 liked families are consolidated into 47 visual and historical lineages to avoid repeating the same recommendation hundreds of times.

### R01 — Garamond and Renaissance book serif

- **Genre:** Serif
- **Liked-family count:** 31
- **Closest Adobe Fonts option:** Garamond Premier / Minion 3
- **Other paid option:** Lyon Text
- **Confidence:** High
- **Upgrade score:** 5/5
- **Why it matches:** Oldstyle modulation, calligraphic stress, literary texture, and strong long-form reading behavior.
- **What differs:** Garamond Premier is more historically specific; Minion is more neutral and systematic; distressed Fell faces retain much rougher period flavor.
- **Liked families represented:** EB Garamond; Cormorant; Cormorant Garamond; Cormorant SC; Cormorant Unicase; Crimson Pro; Crimson Text; Alegreya; Cardo; Gentium Book Plus; Gentium Plus; Charis SIL; Libertinus Sans; Source Serif 4; Vollkorn; Vollkorn SC; Lusitana; Labrada; Poltawski Nowy; Yrsa; Noto Serif; Quattrocento; IM Fell DW Pica; IM Fell DW Pica SC; IM Fell Double Pica; IM Fell Double Pica SC; IM Fell English; IM Fell English SC; IM Fell Great Primer; IM Fell Great Primer SC; Arima
- **Sources:** [Adobe Fonts](https://fonts.adobe.com/fonts/garamond-premier) · [Other commercial source](https://commercialtype.com/catalog/lyon)

- **Closest Adobe Fonts option:** Acumin family / Univers Next
- **Other paid option:** Graphik Collection
- **Confidence:** High
- **Upgrade score:** 4/5
- **Why it matches:** Coordinated weight and width ranges suitable for systems rather than one-off display use.
- **What differs:** Acumin and Univers are more neutral; some liked families have overt display quirks or variable axes not mirrored exactly.
- **Liked families represented:** Encode Sans; Encode Sans Condensed; Encode Sans Expanded; Encode Sans SC; Encode Sans Semi Condensed; Encode Sans Semi Expanded; Barlow; Big Shoulders; Pathway Extreme; Science Gothic; Sofia Sans; Sofia Sans Condensed; Sofia Sans Extra Condensed; Sofia Sans Semi Condensed; Anek Latin; Roboto Flex; Zalando Sans Expanded; Zalando Sans SemiExpanded
- **Sources:** [Adobe Fonts](https://fonts.adobe.com/fonts/acumin) · [Other commercial source](https://commercialtype.com/catalog/graphik)

### I02 — Indic serif and calligraphic display

- **Genre:** Indic
- **Liked-family count:** 17
- **Closest Adobe Fonts option:** Adobe Devanagari / Tiro Indic families
- **Other paid option:** Kohinoor multiscript retail families
- **Confidence:** Medium
- **Upgrade score:** 4/5
- **Why it matches:** Modulated, literary, calligraphic, or display-oriented Indic construction.
- **What differs:** This is not one genre across scripts; Bengali, Gujarati, Gurmukhi, Malayalam, and Devanagari require separate specimen review.

### R02 — Caslon and English oldstyle serif

- **Genre:** Serif
- **Liked-family count:** 16
- **Closest Adobe Fonts option:** Adobe Caslon / King's Caslon
- **Other paid option:** Williams Caslon Text
- **Confidence:** High
- **Upgrade score:** 4/5
- **Why it matches:** English oldstyle proportions, sturdy serifs, and an unforced editorial voice.
- **What differs:** Adobe Caslon is optimized for text and more historically grounded; display members in this group can be sharper or more idiosyncratic.
- **Liked families represented:** Libre Caslon Display; Libre Caslon Text; Sorts Mill Goudy; Average; Judson; Bentham; Balthazar; Cambo; Caladea; Esteban; Jacques Francois; Mate; Ovo; Quando; Quattrocento; Trocchi

### S06 — Soft and rounded sans

- **Liked-family count:** 16
- **Closest Adobe Fonts option:** Proxima Soft / Haboro Soft
- **Other paid option:** VAG Rounded
- **Confidence:** High
- **Upgrade score:** 4/5
- **Why it matches:** Rounded terminals, generous counters, and a friendly low-friction tone.
- **What differs:** Proxima Soft is cleaner and more systematic; Haboro Soft has more personality; playful families can be substantially looser.

### H02 — Comic lettering and energetic hand display

- **Liked-family count:** 15
- **Closest Adobe Fonts option:** Comicraft / Blambot / GoodKitty
- **Other paid option:** Blambot comic families
- **Confidence:** High
- **Upgrade score:** 4/5
- **Why it matches:** Purposeful irregularity, lively rhythm, and stronger weight or bounce than ordinary handwriting.
- **What differs:** Comicraft and Blambot families are usually more genre-specific and professionally expanded; Shantell is more systematic and variable.

### S05 — Futura and Avant Garde lineage

- **Genre:** Sans serif
- **Liked-family count:** 15
- **Closest Adobe Fonts option:** Futura PT / ITC Avant Garde Gothic
- **Other paid option:** Circular
- **Confidence:** High
- **Upgrade score:** 4/5
- **Why it matches:** Single-storey geometric forms, circular bowls, and display-friendly capitals.
- **What differs:** Futura is sharper and more classical; Avant Garde is wider and more stylized; these modern families often have friendlier spacing.
- **Liked families represented:** Jost; Raleway; Raleway; Geologica; Krona One; Syne; Unbounded; Anta; Anybody; League Spartan; Montserrat Alternates; Google Sans; Google Sans Flex; Sofia Sans; Sofia Sans Semi Condensed

- **Other paid option:** Eckmannpsych
- **Confidence:** Medium
- **Upgrade score:** 4/5

| Ultra | Serif | Latin/default | Curated | R07 | Collegiate and heavy display slab | Rockwell Nova / Sutro / HWT Collection | Sentinel / Clarendon | High | Heavy square serifs, low contrast, and poster or collegiate authority. | Rockwell is cleaner and more geometric; Sutro and HWT options are more decorative; Alfa Slab has a denser, more exaggerated color. | Astigmatic | [Adobe](https://fonts.adobe.com/search/fonts?query=Rockwell%20Nova) · [Other](https://www.typography.com/fonts/sentinel/design-notes) |
| Unbounded | Sans Serif | Latin/default | Curated | S05 | Futura and Avant Garde lineage | Futura PT / ITC Avant Garde Gothic | Circular | High | Single-storey geometric forms, circular bowls, and display-friendly capitals. | Futura is sharper and more classical; Avant Garde is wider and more stylized; these modern families often have friendlier spacing. | NaN | [Adobe](https://fonts.adobe.com/fonts/futura-pt) · [Other](https://lineto.com/typefaces/circular) |
| Uncial Antiqua | Display | Latin/default | Curated | D01 | Arts and Crafts, Art Nouveau, and medieval display | P22 Arts and Crafts / ITC Benguiat | Eckmannpsych | Medium | Decorative historical references, uneven stress, medieval or Jugendstil flavor. | The group spans several periods; P22 Arts and Crafts is cleaner, Benguiat is 1970s Art Nouveau revival, and blackletter members need a separate cut. | Astigmatic | [Adobe](https://fonts.adobe.com/search/fonts?query=P22%20Arts%20and%20Crafts) · [Other](https://ohnotype.co/fonts/eckmannpsych) |
| Unlock | Display | Latin/default | Curated | D09 | Psychedelic and experimental decorative display | Cheee / Tomarik / HWT Collection | Digestive / Eckmannpsych | Loose | Expressive display faces driven by distortion, texture, or unconventional construction rather than a shared historical model. | No single substitute is credible for this whole group; recommendations identify premium libraries worth searching, not metric replacements. | Eduardo Tunni | [Adobe](https://fonts.adobe.com/search/fonts?query=Cheee) · [Other](https://ohnotype.co/fonts/digestive) |
